), we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{q=1}^{5}\tilde{S}_{q}+\mathcal{B}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}},

(17)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{1}r_{0}N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}(r_{0}r_{1}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{Z_{i}=1}+r_{0}^{2}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{Z_{i}=1}+r_{1}^{2}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{Z_{i}=0}+r_{0}r_{1}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{Z_{i}=0})^{2}+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}\sum_{k}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}_{jj^{\prime}}\tilde{\gamma}_{j^{\prime}j}.

Note that

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{i}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}(E_{ji}\tilde{\gamma}_{jj^{\prime}})(E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}_{j^{\prime}j})\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\Big\{\sum_{i}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}(E_{ji}\tilde{\gamma}_{jj^{\prime}})^{2}\Big\}^{1/2}\Big\{\sum_{i}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}(E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}_{j^{\prime}j})^{2}\Big\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}E_{ji}\tilde{\gamma}_{jj^{\prime}}^{2}=\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}\sum_{k}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{2}.

As a consequence, we have

\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}\geq\sum_{q=1}^{5}\tilde{S}_{q}.

(18)

Using the equality
r_{1}a_{i}^{2}+r_{0}b_{i}^{2}=(r_{1}a_{i}+r_{0}b_{i})^{2}+r_{1}r_{0}(a_{i}-b_{i})^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{q=1}^{4}\tilde{S}_{q}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{1}N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl\{\sum_{j}E_{ji}(r_{1}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{Z_{i}=1}+r_{0}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{Z_{i}=1})\Bigr\}^{2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}r_{0}}\sum_{i}\Bigl\{\sum_{j}E_{ji}(r_{1}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{Z_{i}=0}+r_{0}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{Z_{i}=0})\Bigr\}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{r_{1}r_{0}}{r_{1}N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl\{\sum_{j}E_{ji}(Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{Z_{i}=1}-Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{Z_{i}=1})\Bigr\}^{2}+\frac{r_{1}r_{0}}{r_{0}N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl\{\sum_{j}E_{ji}(Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{Z_{i}=0}-Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{Z_{i}=0})\Bigr\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{1}N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl\{\sum_{j}E_{ji}(r_{1}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{Z_{i}=1}+r_{0}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{Z_{i}=1})\Bigr\}^{2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}r_{0}}\sum_{i}\Bigl\{\sum_{j}E_{ji}(r_{1}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{Z_{i}=0}+r_{0}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{Z_{i}=0})\Bigr\}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl\{\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}\Bigr\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}r_{1}r_{0}}\sum_{j}\Bigl\{\sum_{i}E_{ij}(r_{1}r_{0}Y_{Z_{i}=1}^{Z_{j}=1}+r_{0}^{2}Y_{Z_{i}=0}^{Z_{j}=1}+r_{1}^{2}Y_{Z_{i}=1}^{Z_{j}=0}+r_{1}r_{0}Y_{Z_{i}=0}^{Z_{j}=0})\Bigr\}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}\Bigr)^{2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}\gamma_{ji}\Bigr)^{2}.

As a consequence, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{q=1}^{4}\tilde{S}_{q}=\sigma^{2}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}},1}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}\Bigr)^{2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}\gamma_{ji}\Bigr)^{2}.

(19)

Comparing with the formula of
\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}})
and by (
18
), we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}-\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}})

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{q=1}^{5}\tilde{S}_{q}-\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}})\geq\Big(\sum_{q=1}^{4}\tilde{S}_{q}-\sigma_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}},1}^{2}\Big)+(\tilde{S}_{5}-\sigma_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}},2}^{2})

\displaystyle\geq-\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{1}{N^{2}}\Big(\theta_{j}E_{ji}+\sum_{k}E_{ki}\tilde{\gamma}_{kj}\Big)\Big(\theta_{i}E_{ij}+\sum_{k}E_{kj}\tilde{\gamma}_{ki}\Big).

(20)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}

\displaystyle\geq\textnormal{Var}(\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}})-\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{1}{N^{2}}\Big(\theta_{j}E_{ji}+\sum_{k}E_{ki}\tilde{\gamma}_{kj}\Big)\Big(\theta_{i}E_{ij}+\sum_{k}E_{kj}\tilde{\gamma}_{ki}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{1}{N^{2}}\Big(\theta_{j}E_{ji}+\sum_{k}E_{ki}\tilde{\gamma}_{kj}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{1}{N^{2}}\Big(\theta_{j}E_{ji}+\sum_{k}E_{ki}\tilde{\gamma}_{kj}\Big)\Big(\theta_{i}E_{ij}+\sum_{k}E_{kj}\tilde{\gamma}_{ki}\Big).

It follows that
\operatorname{E}(2\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}})-\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}})\geq 0
.

Note that, by
Assumption

1
and
Assumption

3
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{1}r_{0}N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}(r_{0}r_{1}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{Z_{i}=1}+r_{0}^{2}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{Z_{i}=1}+r_{1}^{2}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{Z_{i}=0}+r_{0}r_{1}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{Z_{i}=0})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}=O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}\sum_{k}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}_{jj^{\prime}}\tilde{\gamma}_{j^{\prime}j}\lesssim\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}\sum_{k}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}\sum_{k}Q_{jk}^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}\sum_{k}E_{ji}Q_{jk}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{1}=O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

If follows that

\mathcal{B}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}=O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}),

and

\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}=\sum_{q=1}^{5}\tilde{S}_{q}+O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

Since

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}\gamma_{ji}\Bigr)^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}Q_{ji}\Bigr)^{2}=O(N^{-1}),\quad\tilde{S}_{5}\lesssim\sigma^{2}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}},2}=O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

then, we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{q=1}^{5}\tilde{S}_{q}+O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})=\sigma^{2}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}},1}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}\Bigr)^{2}+O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}})+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}\Bigr)^{2}+O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

When
N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}\rightarrow\infty
, we have
\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}=o(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})
. Therefore,

\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}-\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}})=\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}\Bigr)^{2}+o(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

Step 2.
We prove (ii). Recall that

\hat{\tau}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i,j}E_{ij}\Bigl\{\frac{Y_{i}^{\prime}Z_{j}}{r_{1}}-\frac{Y_{i}^{\prime}(1-Z_{j})}{r_{0}}\Bigr\},

with
\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{ij}=\tilde{E}_{ij}{\gamma}_{ij}^{\prime}
. We will prove a general result that encompasses

3
(ii) as a special case. Define
\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}^{\prime}
similarly as
\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}
with

replaced by
Y_{i}^{\prime}
.

Theorem 8
.

Suppose that
Assumption

1
holds for both
\{\alpha_{i},\theta_{i},{\gamma}_{ij}\}_{1\leq i,j\leq N}
and

\{\alpha_{i}^{\prime},\theta_{i}^{\prime},{\gamma}_{ij}^{\prime}\}_{1\leq i,j\leq N}
, Assumptions

–

hold, and for a nonrandom sequence

,

\displaystyle N^{-1}\max\Big\{\sum_{i}\Big(\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime}\Big)^{2},\sum_{i}\Big(\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime}\Big)^{2}\Big\}=O(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}),

\displaystyle N^{-1}\max\Big\{\max_{i=1,\ldots,N}\Big(\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime}\Big)^{2},\max_{i=1,\ldots,N}\Big(\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime}\Big)^{2}\Big\}=o(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

Then, we have

.

with

Proof of
Theorem

8
.

We will only show that

N^{-1}\sum_{i}\biggl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}Z_{j}\biggr)^{2}Z_{i}=\operatorname{E}N^{-1}\sum_{i}\biggl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}Z_{j}\biggr)^{2}Z_{i}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}+N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

The proofs for the convergence of other components in
\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}^{\prime}
follow similar steps.

Simple calculation yields

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\biggl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}Z_{j}\biggr)^{2}Z_{i}=\sum_{i\neq j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime 2}Z_{j}Z_{i}+\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}Y_{j}^{\prime}Y_{j^{\prime}}^{\prime}Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i}.

Define
f(\boldsymbol{Z}):=f(Z_{1},\ldots,Z_{n}):=\sum_{i\neq j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime 2}Z_{j}Z_{i}
. Let
\boldsymbol{z}=(z_{1},\ldots,z_{k}=1,\ldots,z_{n})=(z_{1},\ldots,z_{n})
,
\boldsymbol{z}^{\prime}=(z_{1},\ldots,z_{k}=0,\ldots,z_{n})=(z_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,z_{n}^{\prime})
, we have

\displaystyle|f(\boldsymbol{z})-f(\boldsymbol{z}^{\prime})|=

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{i\neq j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{z})^{2}z_{j}z_{i}-\sum_{i\neq j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{z}^{\prime})^{2}z_{j}^{\prime}z_{i}^{\prime}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{i\neq j}E_{ji}\{Y_{j}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{z})^{2}-Y_{j}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{z}^{\prime})^{2}\}z_{j}z_{i}I(i\neq j\neq k)\Big|+

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{i}E_{ki}\{Y_{k}(\boldsymbol{z})^{2}-Y_{k}(\boldsymbol{z}^{\prime})^{2}\}z_{i}\Big|+\Big|\sum_{j}E_{jk}\{Y_{j}(\boldsymbol{z})^{2}-Y_{j}(\boldsymbol{z}^{\prime})^{2}\}z_{j}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\sum_{j}E_{ji}Q_{jk}+\sum_{i}E_{ki}+\sum_{j}E_{jk}.

By Lemma
4
and
Assumption

3
, we have

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(f(\boldsymbol{Z}))\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\Big(\sum_{i}\sum_{j}E_{ji}Q_{jk}+\sum_{i}E_{ki}+\sum_{j}E_{jk}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\Big(\sum_{i}\sum_{j}E_{ji}Q_{jk}\Big)^{2}+\sum_{k}\Big(\sum_{i}E_{ki}\Big)^{2}+\sum_{k}\Big(\sum_{j}E_{jk}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}+\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}+\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{3}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}.

As a consequence, we have

N^{-1}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime 2}Z_{j}Z_{i}-N^{-1}\operatorname{E}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime 2}Z_{j}Z_{i}=O_{\mathbb{P}}((N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})^{1/2})=o_{\mathbb{P}}(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

Let
\tilde{Z}_{i}=Z_{i}-r_{1}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}Y_{j}^{\prime}Y_{j^{\prime}}^{\prime}Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\Big(Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime}+\sum_{k}\tilde{Z}_{k}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{jk}\Big)\Big(Y_{Z_{j^{\prime}}=1}^{\prime}+\sum_{k}\tilde{Z}_{k}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\Big(Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime}+r_{0}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{ji}+r_{0}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{jj^{\prime}}+\sum_{k:k\notin\{i,j^{\prime}\}}\tilde{Z}_{k}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{jk}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\Big(Y_{Z_{j^{\prime}}=1}^{\prime}+r_{0}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}i}+r_{0}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}j}+\sum_{k:k\notin\{i,j\}}\tilde{Z}_{k}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i}.

Let
F_{ji}=E_{ji}(Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime}+r_{0}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{ji})
and we decompose

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}Y_{j}^{\prime}Y_{j^{\prime}}^{\prime}Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i}=\sum_{q=1}^{6}S_{q},

where

\displaystyle S_{1}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}F_{ji}F_{j^{\prime}i}Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i},

\displaystyle S_{2}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}\Big\{E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}(Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime}+r_{0}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{ji}+r_{0}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{jj^{\prime}})(Y_{Z_{j^{\prime}}=1}^{\prime}+r_{0}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}i}+r_{0}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}j})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-F_{ji}F_{j^{\prime}i}\Big\}Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i},

\displaystyle S_{3}=2N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}F_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}(\sum_{k:k\neq i}\tilde{Z}_{k}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}k})Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i},

\displaystyle S_{4}=2N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}r_{0}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{jj^{\prime}}(\sum_{k:k\neq i}\tilde{Z}_{k}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}k})Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i},

\displaystyle S_{5}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}\neq k}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{jk}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}k}Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i}\tilde{Z}_{k}^{2},

\displaystyle S_{6}=N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}\neq k\neq k^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{jk}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\tilde{Z}_{k}\tilde{Z}_{k^{\prime}}Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i}.

Next, we will show that, for
1\leq q\leq 6
,

\textnormal{Var}(S_{q})=o(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

Step 1
: For
S_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(S_{1})

\displaystyle=N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq j^{\prime}_{1}}\sum_{i_{2}\neq j_{2}\neq j^{\prime}_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}F_{j^{\prime}_{1}i_{1}}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}F_{j^{\prime}_{2}i_{2}}\textnormal{Cov}(Z_{j_{1}}Z_{j^{\prime}_{1}}Z_{i_{1}},Z_{j_{2}}Z_{j^{\prime}_{2}}Z_{i_{2}})

\displaystyle=:C_{1}S_{11}+C_{2}S_{12}+C_{3}S_{13}+C_{4}S_{14}+C_{5}S_{15}+C_{6}S_{16}+C_{7}S_{17}+C_{8}S_{18}+S_{19},

where

\displaystyle S_{11}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}F_{ji}^{2}F_{ki}^{2},\quad S_{12}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{ij}F_{kj},

\displaystyle S_{13}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{jm}F_{km},\quad S_{14}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{jm}F_{im},

\displaystyle S_{15}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}F_{ji}^{2}F_{ki}F_{mi},\quad S_{16}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{ij}F_{mj},

\displaystyle S_{17}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m\neq l}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{mi}F_{li},\quad S_{18}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m\neq l}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{mj}F_{lj},

\displaystyle S_{19}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m\neq l}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{kl}F_{ml}.

We ignore the values of
C_{q}
,
1\leq q\leq 9
, which are all
O(1)
.

We handle these terms
S_{1q}
,
1\leq q\leq 9
, one by one. Note that

F_{ij}\leq CE_{ij},

for some large enough constant
C
. It follows that

\displaystyle\max\{S_{11},S_{12}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}E_{ji}E_{ki}=N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{1}=O(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}),

\displaystyle S_{13}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}E_{ji}E_{ki}E_{jm}\lesssim N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim O(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3}).

To deal with
S_{14}
, let
\xi_{i}=\sum_{j}F_{ji}
. Recall that
\delta_{i}=\sum_{j}Q_{ji}
. Then,

\displaystyle\max_{i}\xi_{i}^{2}\lesssim\max_{i}\Big\{\big(\delta_{i}\big)^{2},\big(\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime}\big)^{2}\Big\}=o(N+N\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}),

which implies that
\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|=o(N^{1/2}+N^{1/2}\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{1/2})
.

We make the following decomposition:

\displaystyle S_{14}

\displaystyle=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}F_{ji}\xi_{i}F_{jm}F_{im}-\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}F_{ji}F_{ji}F_{jm}F_{im}-\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}F_{ji}F_{mi}F_{jm}F_{im}

\displaystyle=:S_{141}+S_{142}+S_{143}.

We handle these three terms separately. For
S_{141}
, we have

\displaystyle S_{141}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{-2}\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}E_{ji}E_{jm}=N^{-2}\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}

\displaystyle=N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}o(N^{1/2}+N^{1/2}\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{1/2}).

By Young’s inequality, for any
0<\delta<2
,

\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{\delta}(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})^{2-\delta}=O\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

then, we have

N^{3/2}\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{1/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}=\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{1/2}\cdot N^{3/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3/2}\cdot\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{1/2}=O\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big)\cdot o(1)=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

If follows that

S_{141}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

For the other two terms, we have

\displaystyle\max\bigg\{|S_{142}|,|S_{143}|\bigg\}\lesssim N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}E_{ji}E_{jm}E_{im}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}E_{ji}E_{jm}=O(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

As a consequence, we have

S_{14}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle S_{15}

\displaystyle=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}F_{ji}^{2}\xi_{i}F_{mi}-N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}F_{ji}^{2}F_{ji}F_{mi}-N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}F_{ji}^{2}F_{mi}F_{mi}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{-2}(\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|)\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}E_{ji}^{2}E_{mi}+N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}E_{ji}^{2}E_{mi}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

\displaystyle S_{16}

\displaystyle=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{ij}\xi_{j}-N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{ij}F_{ij}-N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{ij}F_{kj}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}E_{ji}E_{ki}E_{ij}+N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}E_{ji}E_{ki}E_{ij}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

For
S_{17}
, we make the following decomposition:

\displaystyle S_{17}

\displaystyle=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{mi}\xi_{i}-3N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{mi}F_{ji}

\displaystyle=:S_{171}-3S_{172}+\text{remainder terms},

where

S_{171}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{mi}\xi_{i},\quad S_{172}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}F_{ji}\xi_{i}F_{mi}F_{ji},

and the remainder terms are bounded by
N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}E_{ji}E_{mi}\lesssim N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})
.

For
S_{172}
, we have

\displaystyle S_{172}\lesssim N^{-2}\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|\sum_{i\neq j\neq m}E_{mi}E_{ji}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

For
S_{171}
, we have

\displaystyle S_{171}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}F_{ji}F_{ki}\xi_{i}^{2}-2N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{ji}\xi_{i}

\displaystyle=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}F_{ji}F_{ki}\xi_{i}^{2}-2S_{172}.

To deal with the term
N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}F_{ji}F_{ki}\xi_{i}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}F_{ji}F_{ki}\xi_{i}^{2}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j}F_{ji}\xi_{i}^{3}-N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j}F_{ji}F_{ji}\xi_{i}^{2}=N^{-2}\sum_{i}\xi_{i}^{4}-N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j}F_{ji}F_{ji}\xi_{i}^{2}.

Note that

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j}F_{ji}F_{ji}\xi_{i}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j}E_{ji}\xi_{i}^{2}\lesssim(\max_{i}M_{i})N^{-2}\sum_{i}\xi_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(N^{1/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}+N^{1/2}\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i}\xi_{i}^{4}=

\displaystyle o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

It follows that
S_{171}=o(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})
. Therefore,

S_{17}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

For
S_{18}
, we have

\displaystyle S_{18}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{mj}\xi_{j}-\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{mj}F_{mj}-

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{mj}F_{kj}-\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{mj}F_{ij},

where the last three terms are bounded, up to some constant, by

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}N^{-2}E_{ji}E_{ki}E_{mj}=N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k\neq m}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{mj}\xi_{j}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ki}\xi_{j}^{2}-\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{kj}\xi_{j}-

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ki}F_{ij}\xi_{j},

where the last two terms are bounded, up to some constant, by

\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}N^{-2}E_{ji}E_{ki}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Furthermore, let
(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}=(|\xi_{1}|,\ldots,|\xi_{N}|)
, and we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ki}\xi_{j}^{2}=\sum_{i\neq j}N^{-2}F_{ji}\xi_{i}\xi_{j}^{2}-\sum_{i\neq j}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ji}\xi_{j}^{2},

where

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}N^{-2}F_{ji}F_{ji}\xi_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i\neq j}N^{-2}E_{ji}\xi_{j}^{2}\leq\max_{i}\xi_{i}^{2}\sum_{i\neq j}N^{-2}E_{ji}=\rho_{N}\max_{i}\xi_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle=\rho_{N}o(N+N\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}N^{-2}F_{ji}\xi_{i}\xi_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{j}|\xi_{j}|\sum_{i\neq j}N^{-2}E_{ji}|\xi_{j}||\xi_{i}|\lesssim N^{-2}(\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|)(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}N^{-1}\sum_{i}\xi_{i}^{2}\lesssim o(N^{1/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}+N^{1/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3/2})

\displaystyle=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

As a consequence, we have

S_{18}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Using the same technique, we have

\displaystyle S_{19}=N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq k\neq l}\xi_{i}F_{ki}F_{kl}\xi_{l}+\text{remainder terms},

where the remainder terms are
o(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})
.

As

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq k\neq l}\xi_{i}F_{ki}F_{kl}\xi_{l}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq k\neq l}|\xi_{i}|E_{ki}E_{kl}|\xi_{l}|\lesssim N^{-2}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\sum_{i}\xi_{i}^{2}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

we have

S_{19}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

It follows that

\textnormal{Var}(S_{1})=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Step 2
: For
S_{2}
, we write

\displaystyle S_{2}=

\displaystyle 2N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}r_{0}F_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}j}Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i}+N^{-1}\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}r_{0}^{2}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{jj^{\prime}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}j}Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle 2S_{21}+S_{22}.

Define
g(\boldsymbol{Z})=g(Z_{1},\ldots,Z_{n})=:N^{-1}\sum_{i,j,j^{\prime}}F_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}j}Z_{j}Z_{j^{\prime}}Z_{i}
. Let
\boldsymbol{z}=(z_{1},\ldots,z_{k}=1,\ldots,z_{n})
,
\boldsymbol{z}^{\prime}=(z_{1},\ldots,z_{k}=0,\ldots,z_{n})
and
\boldsymbol{z}_{(-k)}=(z_{1},\ldots,z_{k-1},z_{k+1}\ldots,z_{n})
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\max_{\boldsymbol{z}_{(-k)}\in\{0,1\}^{N-1}}|g(\boldsymbol{z})-g(\boldsymbol{z}^{\prime})|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}|F_{jk}E_{j^{\prime}k}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}j}|+N^{-1}\sum_{i,j^{\prime}}|F_{ki}E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}k}|+N^{-1}\sum_{i,j}|F_{ji}E_{ki}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{kj}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}E_{j^{\prime}k}Q_{j^{\prime}j}+N^{-1}\sum_{i,j^{\prime}}E_{j^{\prime}i}Q_{j^{\prime}k}+N^{-1}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Q_{kj}.

By Assumption
3
, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{k}\Big(\sum_{j,j^{\prime}}E_{j^{\prime}k}Q_{j^{\prime}j}\Big)^{2}=N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{1}=O(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}),

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{k}\Big(\sum_{i,j^{\prime}}E_{j^{\prime}i}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}=N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}=O(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}),

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{k}\Big(\sum_{i,j^{\prime}}E_{ji}Q_{kj}\Big)^{2}=N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}=O(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

Then, by Lemma
5
, we have

\textnormal{Var}\big(S_{21}\big)=O(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})=o((N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})^{2}).

Using
|E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{jj^{\prime}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}j}|\lesssim E_{j^{\prime}i}E_{ji}Q_{j^{\prime}j}
, we can prove in a similar way that

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}\big(S_{22}\big)=o((N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})^{2}).

It follows that

\textnormal{Var}(S_{2})=o((N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})^{2})=o(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

Step 3
: For
S_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(S_{3})=N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\sum_{i_{2}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}\neq l_{2}}

\displaystyle F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\textnormal{Cov}(Z_{i_{1}}Z_{j_{1}}Z_{k_{1}}\tilde{Z}_{l_{1}},Z_{i_{2}}Z_{j_{2}}Z_{k_{2}}\tilde{Z}_{l_{2}}).

We consider only the nonzero terms in the above summation, i.e., the terms correspond to
\textnormal{Cov}(Z_{i_{1}}Z_{j_{1}}Z_{k_{1}}\tilde{Z}_{l_{1}},Z_{i_{2}}Z_{j_{2}}Z_{k_{2}}\tilde{Z}_{l_{2}})\neq 0.
For these terms, we must have
l_{1}\in\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},l_{2}\}
and
l_{2}\in\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}
. We group these terms into
4
categories according to
|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},l_{2}\}|=q
,
4\leq q\leq 7
. We handle these
4
categories separately.

Case 1
:
|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},l_{2}\}|=7
.
There is only one term under this category:

N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l}=S_{31}+\text{remainder terms},

where

S_{31}=N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}}\xi_{i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l},

and the remainder terms are bounded up to a constant by

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{1}=O(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3})=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle S_{31}=S_{311}+\text{remainder terms}

where

S_{311}=N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}\xi_{i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l}\xi_{i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l},

and the remainder terms are bounded up to a constant by

\displaystyle\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}=\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

For
S_{311}
, we have

\displaystyle S_{311}\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l}|\xi_{i_{2}}|E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}\lesssim N^{-2}\sum_{i}\xi_{i}^{2}(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})^{2}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

As a consequence, we have

N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Case 2:

|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},l_{2}\}|=6
.

Case 2.1:

\{k_{1},l_{1}\}=\{k_{2},l_{2}\}
. This includes two cases:
(k_{1},l_{1})=(k_{2},l_{2})
and
(k_{1},l_{1})=(l_{2},k_{2})
.

For
(k_{1},l_{1})=(k_{2},l_{2})
, the summation of the terms under this case is equal to

S_{321}=N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}=S_{3211}+\text{remainder terms},

where

S_{3211}=N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}}\xi_{i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}},

and the remainder terms are considered in the case of
|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},l_{2}\}|=5
in the following proof and have a magnitude of
o(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})
.

For
S_{3211}
, we have

\displaystyle S_{3211}-N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}}\xi_{i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\xi_{i_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{2}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}+N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{2}}.

As a consequence, we have

\displaystyle S_{3211}\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}|\xi_{i_{2}}|E_{k_{1}i_{2}}+N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq i_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{2}}+

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq i_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}|\xi_{i_{2}}|E_{k_{1}i_{2}}+o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-2}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}+o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big)=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

If
(k_{1},l_{1})=(l_{2},k_{2})
, we can verify in a similar way that

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{l_{1}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{l_{1}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}|\xi_{i_{2}}|E_{l_{1}i_{2}}Q_{l_{1}k_{1}}+o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

where the last equality is due to

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}|\xi_{i_{2}}|E_{l_{1}i_{2}}Q_{l_{1}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{E}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}^{\top}\lesssim\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\sum_{i}\xi_{i}^{2}=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Case 2.2
:
\{l_{1},j_{1}\}=\{l_{2},j_{2}\}
, which includes two cases:
(l_{1},j_{1})=(l_{2},j_{2})
and
(l_{1},j_{1})=(j_{2},l_{2})
.

For
(l_{1},j_{1})=(l_{2},j_{2})
, the summation under this case is equal to

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j\neq l\neq k_{1}\neq k_{2}}F_{ji_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l}F_{ji_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j\neq l\neq k_{1}\neq k_{2}}E_{ji_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{ji_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j\neq l\neq k_{1}\neq k_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{ji_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}=O(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3})=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

The proof for the case of
(l_{1},j_{1})=(j_{2},l_{2})
is similar, so we omit it.

Case 2.3
:
\{l_{1},i_{1}\}=\{l_{2},i_{2}\}
.

For
(l_{1},i_{1})=(l_{2},i_{2})
, the conclusion follows from

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}}F_{j_{1}i}E_{k_{1}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l}F_{j_{2}i}E_{k_{2}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq k_{2}}\xi_{i}E_{k_{1}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l}\xi_{i}E_{k_{2}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l}+o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

and

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq k_{2}}\xi_{i}E_{k_{1}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l}\xi_{i}E_{k_{2}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{i}\xi_{i}^{2}N^{-2}\sum_{i\neq k_{1}\neq l\neq k_{2}}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{k_{2}i}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{i}\xi_{i}^{2}N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}=o((N+N\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})N^{-2}N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o((N+N\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Similarly, we can verify the case of
(l_{1},i_{1})=(i_{2},l_{2})
.

Case 2.4

\{l_{1},k_{1}\}=\{l_{2},i_{2}\}
.

For
(l_{1},k_{1})=(l_{2},i_{2})
, the conclusion follows from

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq j_{2}\neq l\neq k\neq k_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{ki_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{kl}F_{j_{2}k}E_{k_{2}k}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq l\neq k\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{ki_{1}}Q_{kl}|\xi_{k}|E_{k_{2}k}Q_{k_{2}l}+o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

and

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq l\neq k\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{ki_{1}}Q_{kl}|\xi_{k}|E_{k_{2}k}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|^{2}N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq l\neq k\neq k_{2}}E_{ki_{1}}Q_{kl}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|^{2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{1}=\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|^{2}(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})N^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

For
(l_{1},k_{1})=(i_{2},l_{2})
, recall that
\delta_{i}=\sum_{j}Q_{ji}
and by
Assumption

4
,

\max_{i}|\delta_{i}|=o(N^{1/2}).

Then,

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{j_{2}l_{1}}E_{k_{2}l_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}|\xi_{l_{1}}|E_{k_{2}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}+

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{k_{2}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}+o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

where the last equality is due to

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}|\xi_{l_{1}}|E_{k_{2}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}|\xi_{l_{1}}|Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\max_{i}\delta_{i}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}|\xi_{l_{1}}|\lesssim o(N^{-3/2})(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{Q}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

and

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{k_{2}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq k_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{k_{2}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}(\max_{i}N_{i})\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|\sum_{k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq k_{2}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{k_{2}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle N^{-2}(\max_{i}N_{i})\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|\sum_{k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq k_{2}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-2}o(N^{3/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})o(N^{1/2}\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{1/2}+N^{1/2})O(N)=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big),

where the last equality is due to Young’s inequality and
N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}\geq C_{+}
.

Case 2.5
:
\{l_{1},k_{1}\}=\{l_{2},j_{2}\}
.

For
(l_{1},k_{1})=(j_{2},l_{2})
We have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{l_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{l_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}+o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

The conclusion follows from

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{l_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{l_{1}i_{2}}Q_{k_{2}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{i}|\delta_{i}|N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{l_{1}i_{2}}=\max_{i}|\delta_{i}|N^{-2}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{i}|\delta_{i}|N^{-2}N^{1/2}\|\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{E}\|_{\operatorname{op}}\|(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\cdot(N^{1/2}+N^{1/2}\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{1/2}))=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

Similarly, we can prove the case for
(l_{1},k_{1})=(l_{2},j_{2})
.

Case 2.6
:
\{l_{1},i_{1}\}=\{l_{2},j_{2}\}
.

For
(l_{1},i_{1})=(l_{2},j_{2})
, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{i_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}\xi_{i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{i_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{1}}+o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

The conclusion follows from

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}\xi_{i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{i_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}|\xi_{i_{1}}|E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle N^{-2}(|\xi_{i}|)_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}=O((N+N\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{1/2})\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

Similarly, we can prove the case for
(l_{1},i_{1})=(j_{2},l_{2})
.

Case 3:

|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},l_{2}\}|=5
. Let
k=\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},l_{2}\}\backslash\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}
. We further divide Case
3
into two cases.

Case 3.1:

\{k_{1},l_{1}\}\neq\{k_{2},l_{2}\}
. If
k=\{k_{2},l_{2}\}\backslash\{k_{1},l_{1}\}
, then we must have
k=k_{2}
, and therefore,

F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}\lesssim E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}.

As
l_{2}\in\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}
, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq k_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\max_{i}|\delta_{i}|\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\max_{i}|\delta_{i}|\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{1}=N^{-2}o(N^{1/2})O(N^{3}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})=o(N^{3/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

If
k\neq\{k_{2},l_{2}\}\backslash\{k_{1},l_{1}\}
, then
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}\in\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}
. Recall that
\bar{\boldsymbol{E}}=\boldsymbol{E}+\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}
and
\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}=\boldsymbol{Q}+\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}
. Since
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}\neq\{k_{1},l_{1}\}
, we have

\displaystyle F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}\lesssim E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}\bar{E}_{qk},~\text{for}~q\in\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq k}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}\bar{E}_{qk}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\max_{i}\{N_{i},M_{i}\}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}o(N^{3/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{2}l_{2}}.

We now bound
\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{2}l_{2}}
. We consider five cases:
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}=\{i_{1},j_{1}\}
,
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}=\{i_{1},k_{1}\}
,
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}=\{i_{1},l_{1}\}
,
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}=\{j_{1},k_{1}\}
,
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}=\{j_{1},l_{1}\}
. Their corresponding magnitudes are as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{i_{1}j_{1}}\lesssim\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{i_{1}j_{1}}\lesssim\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\bar{\boldsymbol{E}}\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}},

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{i_{1}k_{1}}\lesssim\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{i_{1}k_{1}}\lesssim\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\bar{\boldsymbol{E}}\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}},

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{i_{1}l_{1}}\lesssim\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{i_{1}l_{1}}\lesssim\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\bar{\boldsymbol{E}}\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}},

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}k_{1}}\lesssim\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}k_{1}}\lesssim\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\bar{\boldsymbol{E}}\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}},

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}l_{1}}\lesssim\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}l_{1}}\lesssim\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\bar{\boldsymbol{E}}\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}.

Putting together the pieces, we have for
k\neq\{k_{2},l_{2}\}\backslash\{k_{1},l_{1}\}
and
\{k_{1},l_{1}\}\neq\{k_{2},l_{2}\}
those summands are of magnitude
o(N^{3/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})
.

Case 3.2:

\{k_{1},l_{1}\}=\{k_{2},l_{2}\}
. There are two possibilities for
k
:
k=i_{2}
and
k=j_{2}
.

Case 3.2.1:

k=i_{2}
. In this case,
j_{2}=i_{1}
or
j_{2}=j_{1}
. For
j_{2}=i_{1}
, we consider
(k_{1},l_{1})=(k_{2},l_{2})
and
(k_{1},l_{1})=(l_{2},k_{2})
separately.

When
(k_{1},l_{1})=(k_{2},l_{2})
, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{i_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{i_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{2}}\lesssim N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3}.

When
(k_{1},l_{1})=(l_{2},k_{2})
, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{i_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{i_{1}i_{2}}E_{l_{1}i_{2}}\lesssim N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3}.

For
j_{2}=j_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{2}}\lesssim N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3}.

Case 3.2.2:

k=j_{2}
. In this case,
i_{2}=i_{1}
or
i_{2}=j_{1}
. For
i_{2}=i_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq j_{2}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\xi_{i_{1}}E_{k_{2}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}+\text{remainder terms},

where the remainder terms are bounded by

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{k_{2}i_{1}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}\lesssim N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}\lesssim N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}.

The conclusion follows from

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\xi_{i_{1}}E_{k_{2}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|)N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}=o(N^{3/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{1/2}+1))=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

The case of
k=j_{2},\ i_{2}=j_{1}
is equivalent to
k=i_{2}
,
j_{2}=i_{1}
by symmetry.

Case 4:

|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},l_{2}\}|=4
. The summation under this category is equal to

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}F_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}F_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}l_{1}}\lesssim N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}=O(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3})=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Combing Case 1 to Case 4, we have

\textnormal{Var}(S_{3})=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Step 4
. For
S_{4}
, by
Lemma

5
, we have

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(S_{4})\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{\prime}\neq k}E_{jl}E_{j^{\prime}l}Q_{jj^{\prime}}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}+N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j^{\prime}\neq k}E_{li}E_{j^{\prime}i}Q_{lj^{\prime}}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}+

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}E_{ji}E_{li}Q_{jl}Q_{lk}\Big)^{2}+N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}Q_{jj^{\prime}}Q_{j^{\prime}l}\Big)^{2}.

Note that

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{\prime}\neq k}E_{jl}E_{j^{\prime}l}Q_{jj^{\prime}}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}\lesssim N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{\prime}\neq k}E_{jl}Q_{jj^{\prime}}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2},

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j^{\prime}\neq k}E_{li}E_{j^{\prime}i}Q_{lj^{\prime}}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}\lesssim N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j^{\prime}\neq k}E_{li}Q_{lj^{\prime}}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{i}N_{i}^{2}N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq k}Q_{lj^{\prime}}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}\lesssim o(N^{3}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{1}=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}),

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}E_{ji}E_{li}Q_{jl}Q_{lk}\Big)^{2}\lesssim N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}E_{ji}Q_{jl}Q_{lk}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j}E_{ji}Q_{jl}\Big)^{2}\lesssim N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}=o(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}Q_{jj^{\prime}}Q_{j^{\prime}l}\Big)^{2}\lesssim N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}Q_{jj^{\prime}}Q_{j^{\prime}l}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}.

Therefore,

\textnormal{Var}(S_{4})=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Step 5
. For
S_{5}
, by
Lemma

5
, we have

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(S_{5})=

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}E_{ji}E_{li}Q_{jk}Q_{lk}\Big)^{2}+N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{\prime}\neq k}E_{jl}E_{j^{\prime}l}Q_{jk}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}+

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}Q_{jl}Q_{j^{\prime}l}\Big)^{2}.

Since
\max_{i}\delta_{i}^{2}=o(N)
, then we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}E_{ji}E_{li}Q_{jk}Q_{lk}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq k}E_{ji}Q_{jk}Q_{lk}\Big)^{2}\lesssim N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2},

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{\prime}\neq k}E_{jl}E_{j^{\prime}l}Q_{jk}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{\prime}\neq k}E_{j^{\prime}l}Q_{jk}Q_{j^{\prime}k}\Big)^{2}\lesssim N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2},

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}Q_{jl}Q_{j^{\prime}l}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j\neq j^{\prime}}E_{ji}Q_{jl}Q_{j^{\prime}l}\Big)^{2}\lesssim\max_{i}\delta_{i}^{2}N^{-2}\sum_{l}\Big(\sum_{i\neq j}E_{ji}Q_{jl}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{i}\delta_{i}^{2}N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\max_{i}\delta_{i}^{2}=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

As a consequence, we have

\textnormal{Var}(S_{5})=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big).

Step 6
. For
S_{6}
, we have

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(S_{6})=

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}}\sum_{i_{2}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}\neq m_{2}\neq l_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j_{2}m_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\textnormal{Cov}(\tilde{Z}_{m_{1}}\tilde{Z}_{l_{1}}Z_{j_{1}}Z_{k_{1}}Z_{i_{1}},\tilde{Z}_{m_{2}}\tilde{Z}_{l_{2}}Z_{j_{2}}Z_{k_{2}}Z_{i_{2}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}}\sum_{i_{2}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}\neq m_{2}\neq l_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{j_{2}m_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle I(\textnormal{Cov}(\tilde{Z}_{m_{1}}\tilde{Z}_{l_{1}}Z_{j_{1}}Z_{k_{1}}Z_{i_{1}},\tilde{Z}_{m_{2}}\tilde{Z}_{l_{2}}Z_{j_{2}}Z_{k_{2}}Z_{i_{2}})\neq 0).

We only need to consider the terms with
\textnormal{Cov}(\tilde{Z}_{m_{1}}\tilde{Z}_{l_{1}}Z_{j_{1}}Z_{k_{1}}Z_{i_{1}},\tilde{Z}_{m_{2}}\tilde{Z}_{l_{2}}Z_{j_{2}}Z_{k_{2}}Z_{i_{2}})\neq 0
, i.e.,
m_{1},l_{1}\in\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}
,
m_{2},l_{2}\in\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}
. We group these terms into
4
categories according to
|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}|=q
,
5\leq q\leq 8
, and deal with these
4
categories one by one.

Case 1:

|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}|=8
. In this case, we have
m_{1}=m_{2}=m
and
l_{1}=l_{2}=l
, (the case that
m_{1}=l_{2},\ m_{2}=l_{1}
is equivalent, due to the exchangeable role of
m_{1}
and
l_{1}
). Thus,

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}\neq l\neq m}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{j_{2}m}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}\neq l\neq m}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{j_{2}m}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\boldsymbol{1}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{E}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\top}\boldsymbol{1}\lesssim N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{4}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3}.

Case 2:

|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}|=7
. Let
\{l_{1},m_{1},q\}=\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}\cap\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}
. For this category, we consider two cases.

Case 2.1:

\{l_{1},m_{1},j_{1}\}=\{l_{2},m_{2},j_{2}\}
or
\{l_{1},m_{1},j_{1}\}=\{l_{2},m_{2},k_{2}\}
, or
\{l_{1},m_{1},k_{1}\}=\{l_{2},m_{2},j_{2}\}
, or
\{l_{1},m_{1},k_{1}\}=\{l_{2},m_{2},j_{2}\}
. By the exchangeable role of
l_{q}
and
m_{q}
, and
j_{q}
and
k_{q}
,
q=1,2
, without loss of generality, we consider the case
\{l_{1},m_{1},j_{1}\}=\{l_{2},m_{2},j_{2}\}
.

Case 2.1.1
:
\{j_{1},m_{1}\}=\{j_{2},m_{2}\}
.

For
(l_{1},m_{1},j_{1})=(l_{2},m_{2},j_{2})
, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j\neq k_{1}\neq k_{2}\neq m\neq l}E_{ji_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{jm}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{ji_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{jm}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j\neq k_{1}\neq k_{2}\neq l}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{ji_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l}\lesssim N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{2}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3}.

For
(l_{1},m_{1},j_{1})=(l_{2},j_{2},m_{2})
, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j\neq m\neq k_{1}\neq k_{2}\neq l}E_{ji_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{jm}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{mi_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{mj}Q_{k_{2}l}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq j\neq m\neq k_{1}\neq k_{2}\neq l}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l}E_{mi_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{mj}Q_{k_{2}l}\lesssim N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}\lesssim N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3}.

Case 2.1.2
:
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{j_{1},l_{1}\}
or
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{m_{1},l_{1}\}
.

In this case, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{j_{2}m_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{2}\neq k_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}Q_{j_{2}m_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\max_{i}\delta_{i})(\max_{i}N_{i})N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}Q_{j_{2}m_{2}}=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}},

where the last equality holds because
N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}Q_{j_{2}m_{2}}
, under
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{j_{1},l_{1}\}
and
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{m_{1},l_{1}\}
, are bounded, respectively, by

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}l_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}l_{1}}\lesssim N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|_{\operatorname{op}}\lesssim\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}},

and

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{m_{1}l_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{m_{1}l_{1}}\lesssim N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|_{\operatorname{op}}\lesssim\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}.

Case 2.2
:
\{l_{1},m_{1},j_{1}\}\neq\{l_{2},m_{2},j_{2}\}
,
\{l_{1},m_{1},j_{1}\}\neq\{l_{2},m_{2},k_{2}\}
,
\{l_{1},m_{1},k_{1}\}\neq\{l_{2},m_{2},j_{2}\}
, and
\{l_{1},m_{1},k_{1}\}\neq\{l_{2},m_{2},j_{2}\}
.

In this case, we have
\{j_{1},k_{1}\}=\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}\backslash\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}
or
\{j_{2},k_{2}\}=\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}\backslash

\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}
. By symmetry, without loss of generality, suppose that
\{j_{2},k_{2}\}=\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}\backslash

\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}
. Then,

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{j_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{j_{2}m_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{j_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}Q_{j_{2}m_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\max_{i}\delta_{i}^{2}\sum_{j_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq i_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq j_{2}\neq k_{2}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{i}\delta_{i}^{2}N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{2}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|^{2}_{\operatorname{op}}=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

Case 3
:
|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}|=6
. Let
q=\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}\backslash

\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}
.

Case 3.1
:
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}\neq\{j_{1},m_{1}\}
and
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}\neq\{k_{1},l_{1}\}
; or
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}\neq\{j_{1},m_{1}\}
and
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}\neq\{k_{1},l_{1}\}
. Without loss of generality, let’s consider
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}\neq\{j_{1},m_{1}\}
and
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}\neq\{k_{1},l_{1}\}
.

Case 3.1.1
:
q\in\{j_{2},m_{2}\}
.

In this case, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq q}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j_{2}m_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{i}|\delta_{i}|N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{i}|\delta_{i}|N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{2}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|^{2}_{\operatorname{op}}=o(N^{3/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

Case 3.1.2
:
q\not\in\{j_{2},m_{2}\}
.

In this case, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq q}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j_{2}m_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\max_{i}\{M_{i},N_{i}\}N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{2}m_{2}}.

We consider five cases:
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{m_{1},i_{1}\}
,
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{m_{1},k_{1}\}
,
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{m_{1},l_{1}\}
,
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{j_{1},i_{1}\}
,
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{j_{1},k_{1}\}
. We omit other cases due to symmetry. Under those cases, we can bound
N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{2}m_{2}}
as follows:

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{m_{1}i_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{m_{1}i_{1}}\lesssim N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|_{\operatorname{op}}\lesssim\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}},

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{m_{1}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{m_{1}k_{1}}\lesssim N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|_{\operatorname{op}}\lesssim\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}},

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{m_{1}l_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{m_{1}l_{1}}\lesssim N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|_{\operatorname{op}}\lesssim\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}},

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}i_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\lesssim N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|_{\operatorname{op}}\lesssim\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}},

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{j_{1}i_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}}\bar{E}_{k_{1}i_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\bar{Q}_{k_{1}l_{1}}\bar{Q}_{j_{1}k_{1}}\lesssim\max_{i}\{N_{i},M_{i}\}N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{3}_{\operatorname{op}}=o(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}N^{1/2}).

Therefore, the order of the summation under this case is
o(N^{1/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})o(N^{3/2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2})
.

Case 3.2
:
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{j_{1},m_{1}\}
or
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{k_{1},l_{1}\}
; and
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}=\{j_{1},m_{1}\}
or
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}=\{k_{1},l_{1}\}
. Without loss of generality, let’s consider
\{j_{2},m_{2}\}=\{j_{1},m_{1}\}
and
\{k_{2},l_{2}\}=\{k_{1},l_{1}\}
.

In this case, we have

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq q}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j_{1}m_{1}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j_{2}m_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{k_{2}l_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq q}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}^{2}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}^{2}E_{j_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(\max_{j_{1}}\sum_{m_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}^{2}\big)\big(\max_{k_{1}}\sum_{l_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}^{2}\big)N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq l_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq q}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{2}}E_{k_{1}i_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|_{\operatorname{op}}^{4}N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|_{\operatorname{op}}^{3}=O(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{3})=o(N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

Case 4
:
|\{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},m_{1},l_{1}\}\cup\{i_{2},j_{2},k_{2},m_{2},l_{2}\}|=5
.

In this case, we have

E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}E_{j_{2}i_{2}}E_{k_{2}i_{2}}Q_{j_{2}m_{2}}Q_{k_{2}l_{2}}\lesssim E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}},

and therefore, the summation of these terms is bounded by

N^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}\neq k_{1}\neq m_{1}\neq l_{1}}E_{j_{1}i_{1}}E_{k_{1}i_{1}}Q_{j_{1}m_{1}}Q_{k_{1}l_{1}}\lesssim N^{-1}\|\boldsymbol{Q}\|^{2}_{\operatorname{op}}\|\boldsymbol{E}\|^{2}_{\operatorname{op}}=O(N\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}).

As a consequence, we have

\textnormal{Var}(S_{6})=o\Big(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}+N^{2}\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}^{2}\Big)

.

It follows that

\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}=\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}+o_{\mathbb{P}}\Big(N^{-1}\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}+\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}\Big).

∎

D.4
Proof of Theorem
4

Recall that
\hat{\tau}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}=\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{DIR}}}+\hat{\tau}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}
with

\hat{\tau}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i,j}E_{ij}\Bigl\{\frac{Y_{i}^{\prime}Z_{j}}{r_{1}}-\frac{Y_{i}^{\prime}(1-Z_{j})}{r_{0}}\Bigr\},

where
Y_{i}^{\prime}=\alpha_{i}^{\prime}+\theta_{i}^{\prime}Z_{i}+\sum_{j}\tilde{E}_{ij}{\gamma}_{ij}^{\prime}Z_{j}
with
\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{ij}=\tilde{E}_{ij}{\gamma}_{ij}^{\prime}
. We will prove a general result that encompasses
Theorem

4
(ii) as a special case. Denote
\sigma_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime~2}=\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}})
with

\displaystyle\hat{V}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\biggl(Y_{i}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}\frac{Z_{j}}{r_{1}}\biggr)^{2}\frac{Z_{i}}{N^{2}r_{1}}+\sum_{i}\biggl(Y_{i}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}\frac{1-Z_{j}}{r_{0}}\biggr)^{2}\frac{r_{0}Z_{i}}{N^{2}r_{1}^{2}}+

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\biggl(Y_{i}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}\frac{Z_{j}}{r_{1}}\biggr)^{2}\frac{r_{1}(1-Z_{i})}{N^{2}r_{0}^{2}}+\sum_{i}\biggl(Y_{i}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}\frac{1-Z_{j}}{r_{0}}\biggr)^{2}\frac{1-Z_{i}}{N^{2}r_{0}}.

Theorem 9
.

(i)
\operatorname{E}(2\hat{V}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}})\geq\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime})
and (ii) suppose that
Assumption

1
holds for both
\{\alpha_{i},\theta_{i},{\gamma}_{ij}\}_{1\leq i,j\leq N}
and
\{\alpha_{i}^{\prime},\theta_{i}^{\prime},{\gamma}_{ij}^{\prime}\}_{1\leq i,j\leq N}
, Assumptions
2
–
3
hold, then, we have

\operatorname{E}\hat{V}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}=\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}})+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Big(\theta_{i}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}^{\prime}\Big)^{2}+O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

Proof of
Theorem

9
.

Recall the definition of
\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}^{\prime}
as
\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}
with
Y_{i}
replaced by
Y_{i}^{\prime}
.
We make the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}\big(\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime}-\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}^{\prime}-\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{DIR}}}\big)=S_{4}+S_{5}+S_{6}+S_{7},

(21)

where

\displaystyle S_{4}=\frac{2}{N^{2}}\operatorname{E}\sum_{i}Y_{i}Z_{i}\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}\frac{Z_{j}}{r_{1}^{2}},\quad S_{5}=\frac{2}{N^{2}}\operatorname{E}\sum_{i}Y_{i}(1-Z_{i})\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}\frac{Z_{j}}{r_{1}^{2}},

\displaystyle S_{6}=\frac{2}{N^{2}}\operatorname{E}\sum_{i}Y_{i}Z_{i}\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}\frac{1-Z_{j}}{r_{0}^{2}},\quad S_{7}=\frac{2}{N^{2}}\operatorname{E}\sum_{i}Y_{i}(1-Z_{i})\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{j}^{\prime}\frac{1-Z_{j}}{r_{0}^{2}}.

Since

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}Y_{i}Y_{j}^{\prime}\frac{I(Z_{i}=z,Z_{j}=z^{\prime})}{\operatorname{E}I(Z_{i}=z,Z_{j}=z^{\prime})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}(Y_{i}\mid Z_{i}=z,Z_{j}=z^{\prime})Y_{Z_{i}=z}^{\prime Z_{j}=z^{\prime}}+\textnormal{Cov}(Y_{i},Y_{j}^{\prime}\mid Z_{i}=z,Z_{j}=z^{\prime})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}(Y_{i}\mid Z_{i}=z,Z_{j}=z^{\prime})Y_{Z_{j}=z^{\prime}}^{\prime Z_{i}=z}+r_{1}r_{0}\sum_{k}\tilde{\gamma}_{ik}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{Y_{Z_{i}=z}+(z^{\prime}-r_{1})\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}\}Y_{Z_{j}=z^{\prime}}^{\prime Z_{i}=z}+r_{1}r_{0}\sum_{k}\tilde{\gamma}_{ik}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{\prime},

then, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{q=4}^{7}S_{q}=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{i}=1}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=1}+\frac{2r_{0}}{r_{1}N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{i}=1}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=1}+

\displaystyle\frac{2r_{1}}{r_{0}N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{i}=0}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=0}+\frac{2}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{i}=0}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=0}+\frac{2}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}\sum_{k}E_{ji}\tilde{\gamma}_{ik}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{\prime}+

\displaystyle\frac{2r_{0}}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}(Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=1}-Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=1})\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}+\frac{2r_{1}}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}(Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=0}-Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=0})\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}.

Since

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\sum_{k}E_{ji}\tilde{\gamma}_{ik}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{\prime}+r_{0}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}(Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=1}-Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=1})\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}+r_{1}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}(Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=0}-Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=0})\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}\big(\sum_{k}\tilde{\gamma}_{ik}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{\prime}+\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}\theta_{j}^{\prime}\big)=\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}^{\prime}\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}+\sum_{i,j}\sum_{k}E_{ji}\tilde{\gamma}_{ik}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}^{\prime}\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}+\sum_{i,j,k}E_{ki}\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}\tilde{\gamma}_{kj}^{\prime}=\sum_{i,j}\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}(E_{ji}\theta_{j}^{\prime}+E_{ki}\sum_{k}\tilde{\gamma}_{kj}^{\prime}),

then, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{q=4}^{7}S_{q}=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{i}=1}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=1}+\frac{2r_{0}}{r_{1}N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{i}=1}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=1}+\frac{2r_{1}}{r_{0}N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{i}=0}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=0}+

\displaystyle\frac{2}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{i}=0}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=0}+\frac{2}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}\sum_{i,j}\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}(E_{ji}\theta_{j}^{\prime}+E_{ki}\sum_{k}\tilde{\gamma}_{kj}^{\prime}).

(22)

Denote

\displaystyle S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}=S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}},1}+S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}},2},

where

\displaystyle S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}},1}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\biggl(Y_{Z_{i}=1}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=1}\biggr)^{2}+\frac{r_{0}}{N^{2}r_{1}}\sum_{i}\biggl(Y_{Z_{i}=1}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=1}\biggr)^{2}+

\displaystyle\frac{r_{1}}{N^{2}r_{0}}\sum_{i}\biggl(Y_{Z_{i}=0}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=0}\biggr)^{2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\biggl(Y_{Z_{i}=0}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=0}\biggr)^{2},

\displaystyle S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}},2}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}(\tilde{\gamma}_{ji}+E_{ij}\theta_{i}^{\prime}+\sum_{k}E_{kj}\tilde{\gamma}_{ki}^{\prime})^{2}.

Similar to (
17
), we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{q=1}^{5}\tilde{S}_{q}^{\prime}+\mathcal{B}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}^{\prime},

(23)

where
\tilde{S}_{q}^{\prime}
and
\mathcal{B}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}^{\prime}
are defined similarly to
\tilde{S}_{q}
and
\mathcal{B}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}
with
Y_{i}
replaced by
Y_{i}^{\prime}
. Note that

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r_{1}r_{0}N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}(r_{0}r_{1}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=1}+r_{0}^{2}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=1}+r_{1}^{2}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime Z_{i}=0}+r_{0}r_{1}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime Z_{i}=0})^{2}+

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E_{ji}\sum_{k}\tilde{\gamma}_{jk}^{\prime 2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j}E_{ji}E_{j^{\prime}i}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{jj^{\prime}}\tilde{\gamma}^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}j}\geq 0.

By (
21
) and summating (
21
), (
8
) and (
23
), we have

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}+\mathcal{B}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}^{\prime}\geq S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}.

(24)

Since
\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}})=\sigma_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}},1}^{\prime~2}+\sigma_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}},2}^{\prime~2}
with

\displaystyle\sigma_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}},2}^{\prime~2}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\Big(\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}+E_{ji}\theta_{j}^{\prime}+\sum_{k}E_{ki}\tilde{\gamma}_{kj}^{\prime}\Big)\Big(\tilde{\gamma}_{ji}+E_{ij}\theta_{i}^{\prime}+\sum_{k}E_{kj}\tilde{\gamma}_{ki}^{\prime}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\Big(\tilde{\gamma}_{ji}+E_{ij}\theta_{i}^{\prime}+\sum_{k}E_{kj}\tilde{\gamma}_{ki}^{\prime}\Big)^{2},

then mimicking the way we derive (
19
), we have

S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}},1}=\sigma_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}},1}^{\prime~2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\left(\theta_{i}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}^{\prime}\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}\gamma_{ji}^{\prime}\Bigr)^{2}.

It follows that

\displaystyle S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}=

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\left(\theta_{i}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}^{\prime}\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\Bigl(\sum_{j}E_{ji}\gamma_{ji}^{\prime}\Bigr)^{2}-

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{1}{N^{2}}(\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}+\theta_{j}^{\prime}E_{ji}+\sum_{k}E_{ki}\tilde{\gamma}_{kj}^{\prime})(\tilde{\gamma}_{ji}+\theta_{i}^{\prime}E_{ij}+\sum_{k}E_{kj}\tilde{\gamma}_{ki}^{\prime}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}\geq

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime})-\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{1}{N^{2}}(\tilde{\gamma}_{ij}^{\prime}+\theta_{j}E_{ji}+\sum_{k}E_{ki}\tilde{\gamma}_{kj})(\tilde{\gamma}_{ji}^{\prime}+\theta_{i}E_{ij}+\sum_{k}E_{kj}\tilde{\gamma}_{ki})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime})-S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}},2}\geq\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime})-S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}.

Combining with (
24
), we have

\operatorname{E}(2\hat{V}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}})\geq\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime}).

Moreover, under Assumptions
1
–
3
for both
\{\alpha_{i},\theta_{i},{\gamma}_{ij}\}_{1\leq i,j\leq N}
and
\{\alpha_{i}^{\prime},\theta_{i}^{\prime},{\gamma}_{ij}^{\prime}\}_{1\leq i,j\leq N}
, we have

\displaystyle S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}=

\displaystyle\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\left(\theta_{i}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}^{\prime}\right)^{2}+O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

Similar to the way we derive
\mathcal{B}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}=O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})
in the proof of Theorem
3
(i), we have
\mathcal{B}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{IND}}}^{\prime}=O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})
and then,

\displaystyle\operatorname{E}\hat{V}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle S_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}}+O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}})=\textnormal{Var}(\hat{\tau}^{\prime}_{\scalebox{0.6}{{TOT}}})+\frac{1}{N^{2}}\sum_{i}\left(\theta_{i}+\sum_{j}E_{ji}\theta_{j}^{\prime}\right)^{2}+O(\rho_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}).

∎

Next, we prove
Theorem

10
below.
Theorem

4
(ii) is a special case of
Theorem

10
.

Theorem 10
.

Suppose that both
\{\alpha_{i},\theta_{i},{\gamma}_{ij}\}_{1\leq i,j\leq N}
and
\{\alpha_{i}^{\prime},\theta_{i}^{\prime},{\gamma}_{ij}^{\prime}\}_{1\leq i,j\leq N}
satisfy
Assumption

1
. Under Assumptions
2
–
4
, and for a nonrandom sequence
\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}
,

\displaystyle N^{-1}\max\Big\{\sum_{i}\Big(\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=1}^{\prime}\Big)^{2},\sum_{i}\Big(\sum_{j}E_{ji}Y_{Z_{j}=0}^{\prime}\Big)^{2}\Big\}=O(\Lambda_{\lx@scalerel@obj{N}}),
